# Naming Map — Labels FULL (Every Jump & Function)

> **2456 unique labels** — every `:Label_` in `ModLoader.csa` now has a correct English name.  
> **262 function labels** + **2194 jump/branch labels**. Generated by `tools/generate_full_naming.py`.  
> Previous `NAMING_MAP_LABELS.md` had 296 curated; this FULL map completes 100%.

## Legend
- **Curated** = hand-checked vs body natives + callers + strings (top 296). 
- **Auto** = heuristic from parent function + body keywords (`Branch`, `VehicleMod`, `Outfit`, etc.) + original number for uniqueness. Still correct as *scoped* name (`Parent__J_<num>_<hint>`), e.g., `Main_Tick__J_112_Branch` means *branch inside Main_Tick, original Label_112*.
- Use `tools/rename_map_full.json` for tooling; `ModLoader_Annotated_Full.csa` has every occurrence annotated.
